// Every game function boundary this cartridge has been shown to have: the ones the seed split
// proved, plus 37 starts settled from the boundary evidence -- an interval whose stated start
// was filler rather than a prologue, split at the first word that allocates a frame, and an
// interval whose trailing filler was split off past its final return. The count in this line
// read 3,191 while the file held 3,185 entries; it is the measured count now.
//
